### [201] [OpenMarket: A Synchronized Polymarket-Binance Dataset for High-Frequency Prediction-Market Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26245)
*Gregory Young*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文尝试利用Polymarket BTC二元期权市场与Binance现货市场的微观结构特征进行交易，但未产生可交易的预测优势。作者发布了首个毫秒级跨交易所配对数据集（含727M行数据、2.9M个显式领先-滞后对），并得出核心实证结论：在样本外，基于43个微观结构特征的逻辑回归模型未能超越Polymarket自身订单簿隐含的概率，模拟交易净收益为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Polymarket的预测市场（二元期权）与Binance现货市场之间的微观结构关联，尝试利用跨交易所订单流信息获取交易优势。

Method: 收集727M行毫秒级配对数据，提取43个微观结构特征，构建walk-forward逻辑回归模型，在Polymarket BTC 15分钟二元市场上进行样本外预测与模拟交易。

Result: 模型未能超越Polymarket自身订单簿隐含概率；模拟交易归一化收益为-0.116。发现了跨交易所时间偏移特征（Polymarket报价对Binance大波动的中位响应延迟为347ms）。

Conclusion: 核心实证结果为样本外预测的零结果（null result），但本文的主要贡献在于发布了首个公开的毫秒级Polymarket-Binance配对数据集及可复现的采集与校准流水线。

Abstract: OpenMarket began as an attempt to trade Polymarket's BTC 15-minute binary markets against Binance BTC/USDT order flow. The attempt did not produce a tradable edge: out-of-sample, a walk-forward logistic model over 43 microstructure features does not beat, and slightly underperforms, the probability already implied by Polymarket's own order book, and simulated trading nets -0.116 normalized payoff units per attempted trade under stated fee and slippage assumptions. We release the synchronized corpus and infrastructure that attempt produced and, to our knowledge, the first public millisecond-level Polymarket BTC / Binance BTC-USDT paired corpus with explicit pairing metadata. The frozen archive (tag v0.5.2) contains 727,098,247 deduplicated rows across 202 archival snapshots, with event data on 54 observed Polymarket days (57 Binance days) between 2026-02-12 and 2026-05-15, including 2,936,031 explicit lead-lag pairs, alongside a reproducible Rust pipeline for collection, millisecond pairing, Parquet export, and walk-forward calibration. Initial analyses establish Polymarket stylized facts (one-tick top-of-book spreads) and characterize cross-venue timing: an apparent 16 ms median lag on venue source clocks, with relative clock drift bounded to at most 6 ms over the archive but a remaining single-vantage constant-offset ambiguity of approximately plus or minus 99 ms. A synchronization-free event study, measured only on the collector clock, independently shows that Polymarket quotes respond to large Binance moves after a median 347 ms. We position this work as a data-and-methods release whose central empirical result is a null out-of-sample forecasting result.

</details>


### [202] [Multi-Currency AMMs for Decentralized FOREX Markets: Feasibility & Optimal Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26405)
*Reina Ke Xin Li,Andreas Park,Andreas Veneris,Srisht Fateh Singh*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文研究了多货币自动做市商（AMM）的池设计，以最小化交易成本。通过推导封闭式成本公式和最优池权重，并利用层次聚类算法将43种货币分组，发现该设计相比传统的载体货币路由方案可降低约13%的实际交易成本，且收益在金融危机期间保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数货币对缺乏直接流动性市场，国际外汇交易依赖通过主导载体货币进行路由（如通过美元中转）。多货币AMM提供了一种替代方案，通过共享流动性实现直接交易，但需要权衡流动性集中带来的价格影响降低与联合收益风险带来的无常损失增加。

Method: 采用恒定均值AMM架构，推导交易成本的封闭式解，刻画流动性集中与无常损失之间的权衡关系；通过优化池权重最小化成本；将货币分组问题建模为系统级优化，使用层次凝聚聚类算法求解。

Result: 利用2008-2023年43种货币的汇率和交易数据，聚类算法在1.6秒内完成计算，生成具有地理和经济结构特征的货币池。相比载体货币路由方案，实际成本降低约13%，且在全球金融压力时期收益稳定。

Conclusion: 优化的多货币AMM池设计在降低外汇交易成本方面优于传统的载体货币路由方案，且具有计算高效、结构可解释、稳健性强的特点，为多货币流动性市场提供了理论依据和实用方案。

Abstract: Most currency pairs lack a direct liquid market, so international foreign exchange relies on routing transactions through a dominant vehicle currency. Multi-currency automated market makers (AMMs) offer an alternative by sharing liquidity across many currency pairs, facilitating direct cross-currency trade while exploiting liquidity consolidation. This paper studies a multi-currency pool design that minimizes trading cost. Under a constant-mean AMM architecture, equilibrium trading costs reflect the trade-off between reduced price impact from consolidated liquidity and increased impermanent loss from joint return risk. This work derives closed-form costs, characterizes optimal pool weights, and shows that the optimized multi-currency pool dominates the status quo over a range of market parameters. It then formulates the system-level problem of partitioning currencies into multi-currency pools, which is solved using a hierarchical agglomerative clustering algorithm. Empirically, using exchange rate and trade data for 43 currencies over 2008-2023, the algorithm runs in 1.6 seconds and produces pools with geographic and economic structure. Notably, this reduces realized costs by ~13% relative to the status quo of vehicle-currency routing, with gains stable through episodes of global financial stress.

</details>


### [203] [Herding, Momentum, and Reversal in China's A-Share Market: An Agent-Based Network Model with Information Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27063)
*Jiahao Weng*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文构建基于主体的金融市场模型，通过局部羊群行为与延迟信息扩散的联合效应解释股价动量与反转现象，并在中国A股市场进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对动量与反转的解释机制尚不统一，缺乏将行为模仿与信息传播区分开来的量化模型，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用基于主体的模型，投资者在冯·诺依曼/摩尔晶格及ER/WS网络上形成异质高斯信念，结合有限速度信息扩散机制进行模拟；实证采用CSAD、LSV及Johnson S_U变换后的滚动尾部羊群指标分析中国A股市场。

Result: 强羊群效应产生空间聚集交易、大幅价格波动和超额峰度；信息扩散加速价格趋近信号价值，但与社会强化结合会导致超调与反转；中国A股实证中多种羊群指标时变特征一致，在市场动荡期显著上升。

Conclusion: 信息延迟、局部社会强化及羊群效应的衰减是动量与反转的互补机制。

Abstract: This study develops an agent-based financial market model to explain stock-price momentum and reversal through the joint effects of local herding and delayed information diffusion. Investors form heterogeneous Gaussian beliefs about the next-period price, choose among buying, selling, and remaining inactive, and revise their action probabilities in response to neighboring investors. The local interaction structure is represented by von Neumann and Moore lattices and is later replaced by Erdős--Rényi and Watts--Strogatz networks for robustness. A separate information process updates investor beliefs through a finite-speed diffusion mechanism, allowing informational adjustment to be distinguished from behavioral imitation. The simulations show that stronger herding produces spatially clustered trading, larger price fluctuations, and more pronounced excess kurtosis in returns. Faster information diffusion reduces the time required for prices to approach the signal-implied value, whereas the combination of information diffusion and social reinforcement generates overshooting and subsequent reversal. An empirical application to China's A-share market compares conventional CSAD and LSV measures with a rolling tail-based herding indicator obtained after Johnson $S_U$ transformation. The indicators display similar time variation and rise during major market disruptions. These findings identify information delay, local social reinforcement, and the eventual decay of herding as complementary mechanisms behind momentum and reversal.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Bitcoin Runs on a Clock: Why Every Price Indicator Dies and the Halving Clock Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26188)
*Josh Molnar*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 比特币周期指标失效具有结构性规律：从精确→提前→沉默；每次减半后顶部位置稳定在525-546天，底部在364-406天；存在因果幂律（指数~5.6），预注册了2026年底部和下次减半后顶部的时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何曾经精确的比特币周期指标（Pi Cycle、MVRV、Mayer、Puell等）逐渐失效，揭示这并非偶然退化而是一个统一的结构性现象，并寻找真正稳定的周期规律。

Method: 使用固定机械规则（非实时记录）回溯识别周期顶底；构造时序无关零假设检验（5e-6至1e-3）；采用区块自举（block-bootstrapped）路径检验（10,000次）；利用HAC推断进行轮换零假设检验；跨资产（以太坊）和跨数据源验证幂律；M2和收益率曲线宏观因子对比。

Result: 四个减半周期中，五项顶部振荡器的峰值单调递减、谷底升高，阈值失效；短期指标衰减至零甚至符号反转；但比特币内部时间结构稳定：成熟周期顶部在减半后525/546/534天，底部在顶部后406/364/366天。确定性构造下10,000次区块自举从未重现顶部聚类（0/10,000），底部聚类部分由回撤过程内在产生（31-43%）。幂律（指数约5.6）是唯一符号稳定且跨资产复现的信号，在当前周期持有优势。HAC检验显示显著过拒绝（名义0.05实际0.33）。

Conclusion: 传统周期指标的结构性失效不可逆，比特币真正的规律在于绝对时间尺度上的周期相位对齐（特别是顶部），而非价格比例指标。幂律时滞信号在多个维度上稳健，但需未来周期验证。研究预注册了可证伪的窗口，为后续研究提供了可检验的假设。

Abstract: Every widely followed Bitcoin cycle indicator (Pi Cycle, MVRV, Mayer, Puell) called turns precisely for a decade, then degraded in one sequence: precise, then early, then silent. This is one structural phenomenon. Across the four halving epochs (2011-2026), the per-cycle maxima of five top-calling oscillators decline monotonically while minima end higher, so any threshold calibrated on past cycles must stop firing; short-horizon indicators decay toward zero and several invert sign; yet Bitcoin's time structure stays fixed, with mature-cycle tops 525/546/534 days after their halvings and bottoms 406/364/366 days after their tops. Turns are identified retrospectively by a fixed mechanical rule, not a real-time record. Timing-free nulls put the joint clustering at 5e-6 to 1e-3 across every variant. A harder empirical null (block-bootstrapped paths under the identical rule) never reproduces the top cluster under its deterministic construction (0 of 10,000); the bottom cluster is largely intrinsic to the drawdown process (31-43% of paths), so the evidence concentrates in top phase-alignment. In block height (the exact 210,000-block unit) the top null stays 0 of 10,000 and partial bottom structure emerges; shape and volatility overlays do not improve. A causal power law in time-since-genesis (exponent near 5.6) is the only signal whose sign is stable across mature epochs, replicates on a second source and Ethereum, and whose timing edge over buy-and-hold turns positive in the current cycle (one holdout, suggestive not decisive). We rest nothing on per-epoch significance: a rotation null shows HAC inference over-rejects here (size 0.33 at nominal 0.05; p=0.21). Macro drivers (M2, yield curve) show the same instability and lose a joint horse race. We pre-register falsifiable windows: a 2026 bottom (Oct 5-Nov 16) and a next top 525-546 days after the following halving.

</details>


### [205] [Where does the criticality live? Early-warning signals are event-heterogeneous across seven crypto-perpetual liquidation cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27070)
*Ramon Marc Garcia Seuma*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本研究分析2022-2025年七次BTC清算级联事件，发现价格变量在5/7次事件中有临界减速信号，但在两次突发新闻冲击中缺失；杠杆率只在2025年10月异常事件中体现信号；唯一稳健的先兆是做市商订单流方差的压缩，但仅为群体层面而非逐事件预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验加密永续合约清算级联是否具有可复现的临界转变早期预警指纹，并确定关键状态变量。

Method: 使用分钟级价格与5分钟级杠杆/订单流数据，计算去趋势残差的滚动方差和滞后1自相关，通过Kendall-tau统计检验其级联前趋势，对每个事件每个变量执行39种分析配置扫描。

Result: 价格在5/7事件中有临界减速信号，杠杆率仅在2025年10月异常事件中承载信号；做市商订单流方差压缩通过300次安慰剂检验（Fisher组合p≈5e-6），但仅为群体层面规律。

Conclusion: 加密衍生品中单事件临界减速主张脆弱；失败模式本身具有诊断价值——减速缺失恰好发生在最突然的去稳定机制中，暗示这些级联是非连续的冲击驱动转变而非临界转变。

Abstract: Do crypto perpetual-futures crashes carry a reproducible early-warning fingerprint of a critical transition, and in which state variable? We study seven major BTC liquidation cascades (2022-2025, including the record 19B USD event of 10 October 2025) using minute-level price and 5-minute leverage/order-flow data. On detrended residuals we compute rolling variance and lag-1 autocorrelation and test their pre-cascade trend with the Kendall-tau statistic, sweeping 39 analysis configurations per variable per event. No variable is event-invariant. Price carries the critical-slowing-down signature in five of seven events but is silent in exactly the two sudden-news (tariff) shocks, suggesting a two-type structure: endogenous-buildup versus exogenous-shock cascades. The October 2025 event, whose in-sample analysis suggests the signature lives in leverage rather than price, turns out to be the outlier, not the rule. The one regularity surviving all events with data is a compression of taker order-flow variance, which passes a 300-onset placebo test (Fisher-combined p ~ 5e-6) but is a population-level precursor, not a per-event alarm. Single-event critical-slowing-down claims in crypto derivatives are therefore fragile by construction. We argue the pattern of failures is itself diagnostic: slowing down is absent exactly where the destabilising mechanism is most abrupt, as one would expect if these cascades are discontinuous, shock-driven transitions rather than critical ones.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Optimal sizing of a hydrogen-based direct reduced iron-electric arc furnace system integrated with methanol synthesis toward zero-carbon steel production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26270)
*Qiang Ji,Fashun Shi,Lin Cheng,Yeye Xie,Yufei Xi,Zhen Dai,Xun Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于分数规划的H2-DRI-EAF-MeOH系统优化配置模型，通过解耦小时生产速率与年产能、采用年化净投资回报率（ANROI）作为目标函数，实现了在可再生能源波动性下的合理容量配置，案例表明该方法相比传统年化净回报基准可获得更高内部收益率（最高20.67%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源的波动性和间歇性会导致系统容量过度配置，而传统年化成本和度电成本指标无法充分评估投资回报。

Method: 建立分数规划优化配置模型，将小时生产速率限制与年产能解耦为独立决策变量，采用年化净投资回报率（ANROI）为目标，通过Dinkelbach方法求解混合整数线性分数规划模型。

Result: ANROI优化相比ANR基准获得更高IRR（并网20.67%，离网17.08%）；等效年运行小时从8000h降至4000h时IRR上升、电池储能容量下降；离网零碳约束可实现CO2制甲醇但IRR降至14.99%。

Conclusion: 所提ANROI优化方法可有效平衡投资回报与系统配置，零碳离网场景下的CO2制甲醇虽技术可行但经济性不足。

Abstract: A hydrogen-based direct reduced iron-electric arc furnace system integrated with methanol synthesis (H2-DRI-EAF-MeOH) provides a feasible pathway toward zero-carbon steel production. However, the volatility and intermittency of renewable energy sources (RES) may lead to capacity oversizing, while conventional annualized-cost and levelized-cost metrics are insufficient to evaluate investment return. To address these issues, this paper develops a fractional programming-based optimal sizing model for the H2-DRI-EAF-MeOH system. Hourly production rate limits are decoupled from annual production capacities and formulated as independent decision variables. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that increasing hourly production rate limits expands the multi-period operating feasible region, increases the theoretical upper bound on RES matching, and provides an investment-efficiency condition for increasing production rate limits. The annualized net return on investment (ANROI) is then adopted to represent investment return, and the resulting mixed-integer linear fractional programming model is solved using the Dinkelbach method. Case studies show that compared with the annualized net return (ANR) benchmark, the ANROI-based sizing yields higher internal rates of return (IRR), reaching 20.67% and 17.08% under the on-grid and off-grid scenarios, respectively. As the equivalent annual operating hours decrease from 8000 to 4000 h, the IRR increases from 19.68% to 20.67% under the on-grid scenario and from 12.64% to 17.08% under the off-grid scenario, while the corresponding battery storage capacities decrease by 113.56 and 796.31 MWh. The off-grid zero-carbon constraint enables CO2-to-MeOH conversion but reduces the system IRR to 14.99%, indicating that the resulting revenues are insufficient to offset the additional costs.

</details>


### [207] [Data-Driven Stability and Performance Analysis of Lurye Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26277)
*Sahel Vahedi Noori,Peter Seiler*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出数据驱动条件，仅利用有限轨迹数据（无需显式模型）即可验证离散Lurye系统的内部稳定性和诱导ℓ₂性能，通过二次约束和子空间辨识技术将问题转化为凸半定规划，且无噪声下恢复模型基结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Lurye系统稳定性条件依赖于标称LTI系统的状态空间实现，但在实际中精确模型难以获得。本文旨在仅利用输入/输出/状态轨迹数据直接验证稳定性与性能，消除对显式模型的依赖。

Method: 提出两种数据驱动条件：(1) 利用标称LTI系统的有限输入、输出、状态轨迹直接构建凸半定规划；(2) 通过确定性子空间辨识技术从输入/输出数据重构状态序列（相差相似变换），再应用条件(1)框架。两种方法均使用满足充分激励条件的输入数据。

Result: 在无噪声环境下，两种数据驱动条件恢复与传统模型基方法相同的诱导ℓ₂增益界。通过扇区有界非线性示例验证，两种方法均获得与模型基方法一致的结果。

Conclusion: 证明了仅利用轨迹数据即可实现Lurye系统的稳定性与性能验证，数据驱动条件与模型基条件等价（在充分激励和无噪声条件下），为无模型场景下的系统分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper develops data-driven conditions for certifying internal stability and induced-$\ell_2$ performance of discrete-time Lurye systems. The Lurye system is an interconnection of a nominal LTI system in feedback with a static, memoryless nonlinearity. The nonlinearity satisfies a known set of quadratic constraints on the inputs and outputs. Existing conditions for Lurye systems require a state-space realization of the nominal LTI dynamics. Our first data-driven result instead formulates the stability and performance conditions using finite input, output, and state trajectories of the nominal LTI block. Our second condition removes the need for measured state trajectories by reconstructing the state sequence, up to a similarity transformation, from input/output data. This state reconstruction is performed using deterministic subspace-identification techniques. Both data-driven conditions are expressed as convex semidefinite programs. These conditions, given sufficiently exciting inputs, recover the corresponding model-based Lurye condition in the noiseless setting. The proposed methods are illustrated via a simple example with a sector-bounded nonlinearity. Both proposed methods obtain the same induced-$\ell_2$ gain bound as the model-based approach while using only trajectory data from the nominal system.

</details>


### [208] [Planning Waste-to-Energy-Coupled AI Data Centers Through Grade-Matched Cooling and Corridor Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26324)
*Qi He,Chunyu Qu,Wenjie Zuo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种边界一致的筛选框架，用于评估垃圾发电（WtE）与AI数据中心冷却的耦合系统。该框架将冷却视为一种可通过品位匹配供应的能源服务，而非仅依靠电力驱动。通过考虑热衰减、吸收转换及寄生电力损耗，将WtE厂侧可输出热量转化为走廊级规划指标。案例表明，处理1500吨/日、热值10 MJ/kg的WtE厂可提供约78.1 MWth可输出热，在20公里走廊内产生约53.0 MW冷量和8.0 MWe净节电。系统由运行工况而非单一效率决定，给出了不同距离下的热覆盖、质量调整和净节电极限，并评估了1 GW IT园区在不同场景下的电网减负效果及所需WtE规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心快速增长面临电力供应、互联容量和冷却需求的约束，传统电力驱动机械冷却方式难以持续；需要探索将废热转化为冷却能的新路径，以缓解电网压力并提高能源利用效率。

Method: 建立了边界一致的筛选框架，将冷却视为可通过品位匹配供应的能源服务；通过热衰减模型、吸收式制冷转换效率和寄生电力损耗计算，将WtE厂侧可输出热量转化为走廊级规划指标；定义了三种运行工况（全热覆盖、质量调整、净节电）的极限距离。

Result: 1500 t/d WtE厂可输出78.1 MWth热，20 km走廊内产生53.0 MW冷量和8.0 MWe净节电；全热覆盖约20.9 km，质量调整正收益至约22.9 km，净节电正收益至约44.7 km；1 GW IT园区5 km走廊可减排116.9-264.4 MW电网负荷，需3-148个代表厂或0.6-40个满负荷等效厂。

Conclusion: 该框架可识别WtE耦合冷却在何种走廊距离下可行、何时需要混合运行、以及基础设施规模何时成为约束条件，适用于筛选和比较分析而非具体工程设计。

Abstract: AI data-center growth is increasingly constrained by limited deliverable electricity, interconnection capacity, and cooling demand. This study develops a boundary-consistent screening framework for waste-to-energy (WtE)-coupled AI data-center cooling. It treats cooling as an energy service that can be supplied through grade matching rather than only through electricity-driven mechanical chilling. The framework translates plant-side exportable heat into corridor-level planning metrics by accounting for thermal attenuation, absorption conversion, and parasitic electricity for delivery and auxiliaries. In a reference case, a regulated WtE plant processing 1500 t/day of municipal solid waste at 10 MJ/kg provides about 78.1 MWth of exportable heat. At a 20 km corridor, this yields about 53.0 MW of delivered cooling and 8.0 MWe of net avoided cooling electricity after parasitic loads. The coupled system is governed by operating regimes rather than a single efficiency score. Under baseline assumptions, full thermal coverage extends to about 20.9 km, the quality-adjusted criterion remains positive to about 22.9 km, and net electricity relief remains positive to about 44.7 km. For a 1 GW IT campus at 70 percent utilization and a 5 km corridor, net grid relief ranges from about 116.9 to 264.4 MW across scenarios. The required WtE footprint ranges from about 3 to 148 representative plants, or 0.6 to 40 full-load-equivalent plants at a 25 percent displacement target. The framework identifies when WtE-coupled cooling is corridor-feasible, when hybrid operation is required, and when infrastructure scale becomes the binding constraint. It is intended for screening and comparison, not project-specific hydraulic or plant-cycle design.

</details>


### [209] [Time-delay Control Using a New Nonlinear Adaptive Law for Cable-Driven Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26383)
*Wenbo Gao,Yaoyao Wang,Jiawang Chen,Wenliang Zhang,Hanzhuo Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对绳驱动机器人强非线性和低刚度问题，本文提出了一种基于时延估计的自适应分数阶非奇异终端滑模控制策略。核心贡献是设计了一种含自适应指数项的新型非线性自适应律，能有效抑制噪声引起的抖振并在轨迹反转时保持增益。实验表明该方法在RMSE、ITAE和ISCT指标上均有显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳驱动机器人存在强非线性和低结构刚度，在时变不确定性和外部扰动下精确控制极具挑战，现有方法难以兼顾自适应调节速度与抖振抑制。

Method: 在基于时延估计的无模型框架下，结合分数阶非奇异终端滑模误差动力学和快速终端滑模趋近律构建鲁棒控制器；核心创新是设计了一种含自适应指数项的非线性自适应律，用于动态调节更新增益。

Result: 与基准方法相比，两个关节的RMSE分别降低34.52%和31.11%，ITAE降低33.79%和32.97%，ISCT降低6.69%和17.77%。与最新自适应律对比，所提方法自适应响应更快、增益演化更稳定、抖振抑制更优。负载测试验证了鲁棒性和可重复性。

Conclusion: 所提出的TDE-AFONTSM控制策略有效解决了绳驱动机器人的精确控制难题，新型自适应律在调节速度、增益稳定性和抖振抑制方面均优于现有方法，具有良好鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Cable-driven manipulators exhibit strong nonlinearities and low structural stiffness, which make precise control challenging under time-varying uncertainties and external disturbances. This paper presents a time-delay-estimation (TDE)-based adaptive fractional-order nonsingular terminal sliding mode (AFONTSM) control strategy for cable-driven robots. A robust controller is constructed within a TDE-based model-free framework by combining fractional-order nonsingular terminal sliding mode error dynamics with a fast terminal sliding mode reaching law. The main contribution is a new adaptive law that introduces an adaptive exponential term into the update gain to form a nonlinear adaptive mechanism. This design improves adaptive regulation under different operating conditions by suppressing noise-induced chattering during smooth tracking while preserving or enhancing the adaptive gain during trajectory reversal. Lyapunov analysis proves the ultimate uniform boundedness of the tracking error. Experimental results show that, compared with the baseline method, the proposed controller reduces RMSE by 34.52% and 31.11%, ITAE by 33.79% and 32.97%, and ISCT by 6.69% and 17.77% for the two joints, respectively. Further comparisons with recently reported adaptive laws demonstrate that the proposed law provides faster adaptive response, more stable gain evolution, and improved chattering suppression. Additional payload tests further verify the robustness and repeatability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [210] [Fully distributed singularity-free prescribed-time stabilization of the continuous-time generalized adaptive Bellman-Ford algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26424)
*Yuanqiu Mo,Jian Qin,Soura Dasgupta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对广义自适应Bellman-Ford算法（GABF）缺乏收敛速度分析的局限，提出了两种控制策略，实现了在用户预设时间内收敛到稳态值，并通过机器人路径规划等仿真验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GABF协议研究仅关注渐近稳定性，未提供收敛速度信息，限制了其在实际应用中的可用性。

Method: 提出两种控制策略，实现GABF在用户自定义时间（prescribed-time）内稳定收敛到稳态值。

Result: 通过机器人机械臂路径规划（基于真实数据）和基于学习的路径规划等仿真场景，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制策略成功将GABF的收敛时间从渐近性提升至用户可预设的时间范围，拓展了其在时间受限场景下的应用可行性。

Abstract: Building upon the well-established distributed biased min-consensus protocol, which serves as an efficient approach to address the shortest path problem in a distributed fashion, the continuous-time generalized adaptive Bellman-Ford algorithm (GABF) introduces flexibility by accommodating various forms of distance metrics. This adaptability makes GABF suitable for more complex scenarios, such as time-dependent shortest path problem and robotic path planning. However, existing research on this protocol primarily focuses on asymptotic stability, providing no insights into convergence speed, which limits its practical applications. To address this gap, this paper proposes two control strategies that achieve prescribed-time stabilization of GABF by ensuring its convergence to the stationary value within a user-defined time, thereby broadening its applicability. Simulation scenarios, including robotic manipulator path planning with real-world data and learning-based path planning, are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [211] [Global Exponential Stabilization of the Kinematic Bicycle Model of a Car in Polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26442)
*Velimir Todorovski,Kwang Hak Kim,Alessandro Astolfi,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于极坐标和范围归一化坐标的变换方法，将运动学自行车模型转化为严格反馈形式，并利用非传统反步法设计平滑反馈律，实现全局指数稳定，生成类似人类驾驶的泊车轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动学自行车模型在泊车场景中缺乏有效的稳定反馈律，且由于Brockett条件限制，笛卡尔坐标系下无法实现平滑静态反馈稳定，导致现有设计无法复现真实的泊车操作。

Method: 将系统从笛卡尔坐标变换到极坐标和范围归一化坐标，使动力学呈现严格反馈形式，然后利用非传统反步法（backstepping）设计平滑反馈律。

Result: 实现了变换坐标下的全局指数稳定，生成的泊车轨迹与人类驾驶员行为相似，且完全通过反馈控制实现。

Conclusion: 通过坐标变换绕过了Brockett条件的障碍，为低速泊车场景提供了一种有效的平滑反馈控制方案。

Abstract: At parking speeds, the kinematic bicycle is the prevailing model for car-like vehicles. Yet, despite its wide use, stabilizing feedback laws for this system are scarce in the literature, and existing designs often do not reproduce realistic parking maneuvers. This limitation is inherent to the Cartesian coordinates, where Brockett's condition rules out smooth static feedback stabilization. We bypass this obstruction by transforming the system into polar coordinates together with additional range-normalized coordinates that encode the geometry of human-like parking maneuvers. In the transformed coordinates, the dynamics take a strict-feedback form, enabling a nonconventional backstepping design. We exploit the particular structure to develop smooth feedback laws that achieve global exponential stabilization in the transformed coordinates which in turn generates parking trajectories resembling the one performed by human drivers through feedback alone.

</details>


### [212] [Online Monitoring and Risk Assessment of Non-Cooperative UAVs via STL-Aware Adaptive Fusion Kalman Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26527)
*Xinhao Yan,Ruige Yang,Chao Peng,Hailong Huang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成多模型多速率卡尔曼滤波与信号时序逻辑（STL）的统一框架，用于非合作无人机的在线状态估计与预测性风险评估。该框架通过STL感知的时变模式转换机制，将安全语义直接嵌入模式推理过程，从而提升对机动变化、异步感知和演变威胁的响应能力，并实现早期安全预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时处理非合作UAV的异步异构传感数据、不确定运动模式以及实时安全评估需求，缺乏一个能同时进行底层状态跟踪与高层安全推理的统一框架。

Method: 提出IMM多速率卡尔曼滤波与STL的集成框架，核心创新是STL感知的时变模式转换机制，利用形式化安全规范导出的鲁棒性度量在线更新模型概率，从而实现安全语义驱动的模式推理与状态估计。

Result: 在实时UAV监控平台上，该方法相比传统方法提高了估计精度，并生成了更早、信息更丰富的安全警告。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能有效实现非合作UAV的在线状态估计与预测性风险评估，在实时监控和安全预警应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper considers the problem of online state estimation and predictive risk assessment for non-cooperative unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in the presence of asynchronous heterogeneous sensing and uncertain motion modes. To address this problem, a unified estimation and safety-assessment framework is developed by integrating an interacting multiple-model multi-rate Kalman filter with signal temporal logic (STL). The proposed framework enables simultaneous low-level state tracking and high-level safety reasoning within a common recursive architecture. Its main contribution is an STL-aware time-varying mode transition mechanism that updates model probabilities online using robustness measures induced by formal safety specifications. By embedding safety semantics directly into the mode inference and estimation process, the method improves responsiveness to maneuver variations, sensing asynchrony, and evolving threat patterns. Based on the estimated state distributions, the framework further generates multi-step state predictions and probabilistic reachable sets, which are used for finite-horizon safety evaluation and risk-triggered warning generation. Consequently, the proposed method provides not only estimates of the current target state, but also early indication of unsafe behaviors before they become fully observable. Finally, experimental results obtained from a real-time UAV monitoring platform show that the proposed approach improves estimation accuracy and produces earlier and more informative safety warnings, demonstrating its effectiveness for real-time UAV surveillance and safety monitoring applications.

</details>


### [213] [Emission-Forecasting-Based Spatial-Temporal Carbon Response: A Multi-Agent Attention-Enhanced Deep Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26560)
*Feiyu Cai,Jing Qiu,Yi Yang,Chenxi Zhang,Xinlei Wang,Baichuan Liu,Junhua Zhao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习和大语言模型的前瞻性碳响应框架，通过预测日前节点碳强度并调度空间-时间可调度负荷（移动储能系统和分布式数据中心），在减少1小时调度延迟的情况下实现超过30%的减排效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统节点碳强度采用事后(ex-post)计算方式，导致低碳调度存在延迟，无法快速响应碳强度波动，限制了碳减排效果。

Method: 提出前瞻性(ex-ante)时空碳响应框架，核心包括：(1) 基于深度学习的层级设计，结合双阶段注意力机制和LLM多智能体协作系统，预测日前节点碳强度；(2) 考虑移动储能系统和分布式数据中心的时空碳调度模型。

Result: 在改进的IEEE 33节点系统上测试，结果表明将碳调度延迟减少1小时，可实现超过30%的减排效果。

Conclusion: 本研究突破了被动碳核算的局限性，迈向主动碳管理，为加速清洁电力系统转型提供了智能解决方案，直接支持可持续生产目标。

Abstract: As a major contributor to carbon emissions, the decarbonization of power systems has garnered significant societal attention. Nodal carbon intensity (NCI), a critical factor in carbon-oriented demand response, has traditionally been determined through ex-post calculations. However, this ex-post approach introduces latency in low-carbon dispatch. To address this, this paper presents a proactive ex-ante spatial-temporal carbon response framework. At its core, we develop a novel deep learning-based hierarchical design, enhanced by a dual-stage attention mechanism and a large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent cooperation system, to accurately forecast day-ahead NCI. This design effectively mitigates the impact of renewable energy uncertainty and enhances predictive resilience. On the demand side, the framework proposes a spatial-temporal carbon scheduling model that integrates geographically dispatchable loads (GDLs), including mobile energy storage systems (MESSs) and distributed data centers (DDCs). Leveraging high-accuracy day-ahead NCI predictions, the framework can effectively reduce system emissions by quickly responding to carbon intensity fluctuations. The proposed framework is tested on the modified IEEE 33-bus system. According to the simulation results, the impacts of proposed framework on dispatching latency and emission outcomes are analyzed. The results demonstrate that under a one-hour reduction in carbon scheduling latency, the proposed model and methodology can achieve over 30% emission reduction. This research breaks through the limitations of passive carbon accounting, advancing toward proactive carbon management. It offers an intelligent solution that accelerates the transition to cleaner power systems while directly supporting sustainable production goals.

</details>


### [214] [A Physics-Informed Framework for PID Tuning of Chemical Processes Using Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26594)
*Zhoupeng Shou,Xiaodong Hong,Congjing Ren,Jingdai Wang,Yongrong Yang,Zuwei Liao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语言模型辅助的PID整定框架，利用大语言模型（如DeepSeek-V4-Flash、Qwen3.7-Plus）或小语言模型（Qwen3-0.6B）模拟工程师迭代调试流程，通过IMC基准示范、监督微调和物理信息GRPO优化，在FOPDT/SOPDT测试中实现最高94%的首推成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PID整定依赖辨识模型或工程师手动迭代，缺乏形式化、可自动化的诊断-调整闭环流程，且小模型难以保证整定质量。本文旨在将工程师的迭代调试经验形式化为语言模型辅助框架，兼顾大模型的高能力与小模型的本地部署可行性。

Method: 1) 大模型流程：接收闭环响应特征、控制工程诊断、整定偏好和IMC基准演示，迭代生成并校正PID参数，直至满足验收标准。2) 小模型(Qwen3-0.6B)两阶段训练：第一阶段SFT基于仿真验证的IMC目标；第二阶段PI-GRPO引入不可补偿稳定性与性能奖励进行优化。

Result: 大模型在FOPDT/SOPDT测试中成功率75-89%；Qwen3-0.6B经SFT后首推成功率86.5%，PI-GRPO进一步提升至94.0%，主要改善首推可靠性和稳定裕度。

Conclusion: 语言模型辅助PID整定框架可有效模拟工程师的迭代调试流程，小模型经过针对性训练能在本地部署场景下达到接近甚至超越大模型的整定可靠性，证明了物理信息强化学习在小模型控制工程应用中的价值。

Abstract: PID tuning for chemical processes commonly relies on identified process models, whereas plant engineers often retune loops iteratively by observing responses, diagnosing deficiencies, adjusting gains, and validating the result. This work formalizes this engineer-like workflow in a language-model-assisted PID tuning framework applicable to both large and small language models (LLMs/SLMs). Hosted LLMs receive closed-loop response features, control-engineering diagnoses, tuning preferences, and internal model control (IMC)-based demonstrations to generate and iteratively correct PID gains under common acceptance criteria. For local deployment, Qwen3-0.6B is adapted through supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with simulation-verified IMC targets and physics-informed group relative policy optimization (PI-GRPO) with non-compensable stability and performance rewards. On 100 first-order plus dead time (FOPDT) and 100 second-order plus dead time (SOPDT) test cases, hosted LLMs (DeepSeek-V4-Flash and Qwen3.7-Plus) achieve final success rates of 75-89% and 77-79%, respectively. As for Qwen3-0.6B, supervised fine-tuning raises first-recommendation success to 86.5%, and PI-GRPO further increases it to 94.0%, primarily improving first-attempt reliability and stability margins.

</details>


### [215] [An extended Perron-Frobenius operator filter for nonlinear state estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26632)
*Yuta Miwa,Yoshihiko Susuki,Shunji Kotsuki*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩展Perron-Frobenius算子的非线性状态估计滤波器（PFOF），利用扩展动态模态分解（eDMD）学习非线性系统的无限维线性算子表示，在保持基函数选择灵活性的同时显式处理非高斯分布，数值实验证明了其高计算效率和高估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性状态估计方法（如扩展卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波）在处理非线性系统时面临非高斯分布、计算复杂度高等挑战，需要一种既能保留非线性系统特性又能高效进行状态估计的方法。

Method: 提出扩展PFOF方法，通过学习非线性系统的Perron-Frobenius算子（一个无限维线性算子），结合eDMD进行线性算子逼近，并允许自由选择基函数以适应不同系统特性。

Result: 通过两个数值例子验证，扩展PFOF在计算效率和估计精度方面均表现优异，且基函数选择的灵活性为性能提升提供了空间。

Conclusion: 扩展PFOF有效结合了线性算子表示的自由度与非线性系统的保真性，为非高斯、非线性状态估计提供了一种高效且精确的新方案。

Abstract: We propose an extended Perron--Frobenius Operator Filter (PFOF) for nonlinear state estimation. The method learns the Perron--Frobenius operator, an infinite-dimensional linear operator fully preserving properties of a nonlinear dynamical system, using the extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (eDMD). This enables us to explicitly account for non-Gaussian distributions exhibited by the nonlinear system within a linear-operator representation, while retaining the freedom to choose basis functions in eDMD. Through two numerical examples, we show that the extended PFOF achieves high computational efficiency and high estimation accuracy by exploiting the flexibility in the choice of basis functions in eDMD.

</details>


### [216] [UAV Swarming for Air-Ground ISAC via Cross-Region Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26679)
*Linghui Miao,Shijian Gao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向无人机群的空地一体化感知与通信（ISAC）跨区域协同框架，通过服务驱动的区域划分、自适应握手协议和区域级MAPPO算法，在动态通信需求与有限感知多样性之间取得平衡，实现了约90%的通信QoS和约45%的CRB降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机群在空地一体化感知与通信（ISAC）中面临的两大挑战：地面通信需求的动态不均衡性，以及感知观测多样性的局限性。

Method: 1) 服务驱动的区域划分方案，实现流量感知的无人机通信；2) 自适应握手机制，通过控制同步开销来降低区域间相位误差，提升协同感知精度；3) 基于CTDE（集中训练-分散执行）的区域级多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）框架，实现跨区域协同决策。

Result: 相比传统基线方法，所提方法实现了约90%的通信服务质量（QoS），并将克拉美罗界（CRB）降低约45%。

Conclusion: 跨区域协同框架能够有效应对无人机群ISAC场景中的动态通信需求与感知多样性不足问题，显著提升通信与感知的双重性能。

Abstract: To serve the volumetric air-ground space, uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are urgently needed. Yet, relying on them for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) introduces two key challenges: 1) dynamic and imbalanced ground communication demand, and 2) limited observation diversity for sensing. To address these issues, a cross-region cooperative framework is designed to coordinate UAV swarms. Specifically, a service-driven regional partitioning scheme is proposed to support traffic-aware UAV communication, and an adaptive handshaking mechanism is introduced to improve cooperative sensing accuracy by mitigating residual inter-region phase errors with controlled synchronization overhead. Based on these designs, a region-level multi-agent proximal policy optimization (MAPPO) framework with centralized training and decentralized execution (CTDE) is developed for cross-region cooperative decision-making. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a communication quality-of-service (QoS) of approximately 90% and reduces the Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) by about 45% compared to conventional baselines.

</details>


### [217] [Global Sensitive-Based Input Shaping for UAV-Payload Precision Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26717)
*Karan Baker,Sanjay Maharjan,Tariq Hlayel,Oladapo Ogunbodede,Dutch Dunphy,Adrian Stein*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于全局敏感性和Shapley值的输入整形器，用于3D无人机-载荷系统，增强对载荷质量和绳索长度不确定性的鲁棒性，并通过数值仿真验证了其相比非鲁棒、鲁棒和最小最大设计的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机-载荷系统中存在载荷质量和绳索长度等参数不确定性，传统控制器对这些不确定参数敏感，需要一种能系统性考虑不确定性并增强鲁棒性的控制方法。

Method: 采用全局敏感性分析结合Shapley值概念设计输入整形器，通过不确定性感知的控制框架，在控制器设计中系统性地考虑参数不确定性。

Result: 数值仿真表明，所提出的基于全局敏感性或Shapley值的输入整形器在性能上优于非鲁棒、鲁棒和最小最大设计。

Conclusion: 全局敏感性和Shapley值方法为无人机空中载荷运输中的不确定性感知控制提供了有前景的框架，能有效提升控制器对不确定参数的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive analysis and design of global sensitivity-based input shapers for a 3D Unmanned Aerial Vehicle-payload system, emphasizing robustness against uncertainties in payload mass and rope length. The proposed approach also leverages the Shapley value concept in controller design to systematically account for uncertainties, thereby reducing the controller's sensitivity to unknown parameters. To validate the effectiveness of the methodology, numerical simulations are conducted, comparing the proposed controller against non-robust, robust, and minimax designs. The results demonstrate that the standard global sensitivity or Shapley-based input shapers improve performance and offer a promising framework for uncertainty-aware control in aerial payload transport.

</details>


### [218] [Joint State-Parameter Inference Enhances Estimation Performance in Model-Based Digital Therapeutics for Type 1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26790)
*Milad Banitalebi Dehkordi,Vihangkumar V. Naik,Manas Mejari,Dario Piga,Jose Garcia-Tirado*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Rao-Blackwellized Stein 变分梯度下降（RBSVGD）的递归滤波框架，用于1型糖尿病生理模型的状态与参数联合在线估计，在虚拟患者数据上优于标准 EKF 和 AEKF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的血糖控制系统中，模型参数常被设为固定值或基于群体值定义，但在真实自由生活条件下固定参数会限制血糖水平和状态的准确重建与估计，因此需要一种能在线联合估计状态和参数的方法。

Method: 采用 Rao-Blackwellized Stein Variational Gradient Descent (RBSVGD) 滤波器，计算非线性时变生理模型中状态和参数的联合后验分布，并将其应用于 Hovorka 葡萄糖-胰岛素模型，在 OHSU 模拟器的20名虚拟患者数据上进行验证。

Result: 与标准 EKF（固定参数）和 AEKF（联合状态-参数估计）相比，RBSVGD 框架在血糖估计精度和模型参数估计方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: RBSVGD 递归滤波框架能有效实现1型糖尿病生理模型的状态与参数联合在线估计，显著优于传统滤波方法，为实时血糖控制决策支持提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Blood glucose estimation is the cornerstone of model-based decision support (DS) and Automated Insulin Delivery (AID) systems. Control systems that rely on physiologic/compartmental models depend heavily on model parameterization, which is either defined using population values or personalized through the user's data. Often, the model parameters are defined as constants. However, under real-world free-living conditions, fixed parameters can limit the accurate reconstruction and estimation of glucose levels and states. In this paper, we propose and discuss a recursive filtering framework for online joint state estimation and parameter identification in nonlinear, time-varying physiological models for Type 1 Diabetes (T1D). Specifically, we employ a Rao-Blackwellized Stein Variational Gradient Descent (RBSVGD) filter to compute the joint posterior distributions of model states and parameters. The proposed approach is applied to the Hovorka glucose-insulin model and validated using data generated by the the Oregon Health & Science University (OHSU) simulator across 20 virtual patients. We perform a comparative analysis against: (i) a standard Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) with fixed model parameters, and (ii) an Augmented Extended Kalman Filter (AEKF) for joint state-parameter estimation. The results demonstrate that the proposed RBSVGD-based framework outperforms both EKF and AEKF approaches not only in terms of the accuracy of glucose estimation, but also in terms of estimated model parameters.

</details>


### [219] [Network Verified NTN Positioning for 6G A Standards Oriented Survey of Hybrid TN NTN Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26846)
*Donglin Wang,Zexing Fang,Qiuheng Zhou,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了无线定位从LTE/GNSS向5G Advanced、6G及NTN系统的演进，重点关注3D定位、网络可验证性和标准化进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能交通、无人机运营、低空经济等新兴应用不仅需要高精度定位，还要求垂直感知和验证可信度，推动定位技术向混合、高精度、3D和网络可验证方向发展。

Method: 通过综述LTE/GNSS到NR、Release-18/19增强定位及NTN 3D定位的演进，综合分析了观测族、3GPP标准演进、方法级权衡以及混合TN-NTN设计的开放挑战。

Result: 系统梳理了定位技术从传统蜂窝/卫星向混合高精度的演进路径，揭示了标准化影响、垂直可观测性、可靠性感知测量选择和网络侧验证等关键问题。

Conclusion: 未来无线定位需要融合TN和NTN，在标准化、垂直感知和可靠性验证方面持续突破，以满足新兴应用场景需求。

Abstract: Wireless positioning is evolving from legacy cellular and standalone satellite navigation toward hybrid, high-accuracy, three-dimensional (3D), and network-verifiable frameworks for 5G Advanced, 6G, and non terrestrial network (NTN) systems. This shift is driven by intelligent transportation, unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) operation, low-altitude economy services, public warning, and regulated NTN use cases that require not only accurate location estimates, but also vertical awareness and verification confidence. This survey reviews the evolution from Long Term Evolution (LTE) and Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) positioning to New Radio (NR), Release-18/19 positioning enhancements, and NTN-enabled 3D positioning. It synthesizes observable families, 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) standard evolution, method-level tradeoffs, and open challenges for hybrid terrestrial network (TN) NTN designs, with emphasis on standardization impact, vertical observability, reliability-aware measurement selection, and network-side verification.

</details>


### [220] [From Crop and Energy Data to Optimized Lighting Scheduling: A Surrogate-Based MILP Framework for Vertical Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26968)
*Francesco Ceccanti,Andrea Baccioli,Aldo Bischi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代理模型的优化框架，用于水培垂直农场的照明管理，以降低电力成本。通过将作物-能量响应数据转化为数学规划可用的关系，并应用于意大利北部工业规模垂直农场的生菜种植，优化后的光照调度相比固定照明方案，电力消耗降低最多15.9%，电力成本降低最多17.8%。研究还发现，中等光照强度结合灵活的光周期比高强度照明更具成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直农场虽能实现高产量和稳定生产，但其经济可行性受限于人工照明和气候控制的高额电力成本，亟需一种能够优化照明管理、降低成本的有效方法。

Method: 开发了一种基于代理模型的优化框架，关键贡献在于将作物-能量响应数据转化为适合数学规划的关系。该方法可应用于实验数据或验证过的动态模型输出。通过生成合成数据，推导出鲜生物量增长、叶面积指数及照明、供暖、制冷电力需求的代理关系，这些关系依赖于光合光子通量密度、作物阶段和室外温度。研究了不同时间聚合水平对代理精度、调度灵活性和计算紧凑性的影响。

Result: 代理模型足够准确地复现了参考模型的作物和能量趋势。优化后的调度相比固定照明基准，电力消耗减少最多15.9%，电力成本降低最多17.8%，同时达到目标收获重量。中等光照强度结合灵活光周期比高强度照明更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 提出的框架提供了一种可推广的方法，能够将作物生长、能源需求和电价波动整合到垂直农场的照明策略中，具有较好的实用性和推广潜力。

Abstract: Vertical farming enables high productivity and stable crop production under controlled conditions, but its economic feasibility remains limited by electricity demand for artificial lighting and climate control. This study develops a surrogate-based optimization framework for cost-minimizing lighting management in hydroponic vertical farms. A key contribution is a procedure for converting crop-energy response data into relationships suitable for mathematical programming. The method can be applied to experimental datasets or outputs from validated dynamic models, provided that relevant ranges of control variables, crop stages, and energy responses are represented. The framework is applied to lettuce cultivation in an industrial-scale vertical farm in northern Italy. Synthetic data generated by a verified agri-energy model are used to derive surrogate relationships for fresh biomass growth, leaf area index, and electricity demand for lighting, heating, and cooling. These relationships depend on photosynthetic photon flux density, crop stage, and outdoor temperature. Different temporal aggregation levels are examined to evaluate the trade-off among surrogate accuracy, scheduling flexibility, and computational compactness. The surrogate model reproduced crop and energy trends of the reference model with sufficient accuracy for optimization. Compared with fixed-lighting benchmarks, optimized schedules reduced electric energy consumption by up to 15.9% and electricity cost by up to 17.8%, while achieving the target harvest weight. Results indicate that moderate light intensities combined with flexible photoperiods are more cost-effective than high-intensity lighting under the investigated conditions. The proposed framework offers a tractable and generalizable approach for integrating crop growth, energy demand, and electricity price variability into vertical farming lighting strategies.

</details>


### [221] [Dynamic Storage Operation Under Uncertainty and the Reliability Externality: Implications for Capacity Investments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27021)
*Daniel Shen,Marija Ilic,John Parsons*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究需求不确定性如何影响储能系统的动态运行决策及长期投资结果，发现不确定性会引发预防性储能策略，并与电力市场的可靠性外部性相互作用，扭曲储能运行和投资。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储能系统越来越被用于应对可再生能源波动和电气化带来的短期需求不确定性，但与常规发电机不同，储能的可靠性贡献依赖于运行策略，需要在短期套利与未来稀缺风险之间权衡。作者旨在理解需求不确定性如何改变这种动态储能运行，以及这些运行决策如何传导至长期投资结果。

Method: 将储能运行建模为平均成本马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并将得到的平稳策略嵌入一个简化的容量扩张框架中进行研究。

Result: 需求不确定性会引发预防性储能策略，以对冲随机稀缺风险，导致储能后的需求分布与完美预见基准情形存在显著差异。此外，电力市场特有的可靠性外部性与不确定性交互作用，会独特地扭曲储能的运行和投资决策。

Conclusion: 需求不确定性对储能系统的运行和长期投资有重要影响，预防性策略是关键机制；电力市场可靠性外部性进一步加剧了这种扭曲效应，需要在政策设计中加以考虑。

Abstract: Energy storage is increasingly relied upon to meet short-term demand uncertainties from renewable variability and electrification. Unlike conventional generators, storage's contribution to reliability is policy-dependent and balances near-term arbitrage against future scarcity risk. We study how demand uncertainty alters such dynamic storage operation and how these operating decisions propagate into long-run investment outcomes. We formulate storage operation as an average-cost Markov decision process and embed the resulting stationary policies into a stylized capacity expansion framework. Demand uncertainty induces a precautionary storage policy which hedges against stochastic scarcity, leading to materially different post-storage demand distributions relative to perfect-foresight benchmarks. We additionally demonstrate that the reliability externality characteristic of electricity markets interacts with uncertainty in a manner that uniquely distorts both storage operation and investment.

</details>


### [222] [Some intuition for why cooperative systems "look 1-dimensional" and 2-cooperative systems "look 2-dimensional"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27176)
*Eduardo D. Sontag*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文旨在基于Birkhoff-Hilbert投影度量收缩理论，为合作系统（包括两合作系统）的维度降低现象提供几何直觉解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作系统在不可约条件下表现为一维系统（如Hirsch收敛定理），两合作系统表现为二维系统（如Poincaré-Bendixson型定理）。作者希望为这种维度降低现象提供几何直观理解。

Method: 利用Birkhoff-Hilbert投影度量在正锥上的收缩性质，解释为何在适当条件下高维合作系统的动力学行为会降低到低维表现。

Result: 通过Birkhoff-Hilbert度量的几何视角，为合作系统与两合作系统的维度降低提供了直观的几何解释。

Conclusion: Birkhoff-Hilbert投影度量收缩理论可以有效解释合作系统维度降低的几何本质。

Abstract: It is known that cooperative systems, and more generally systems monotone with respect to cones, behave under appropriate irreducibility conditions like one-dimensional systems, as evidenced by results such as Hirsch's generic convergence theorem. It is also known, following work of Sanchez and others, that two-cooperative systems, those preserving a generally nonconvex 2-dimensional cone (tested through the diminishing of sign variations), behave like two-dimensional systems, as evidenced by Poincare--Bendixson-type theorems. In these notes I attempt to give some geometric intuition for these dimensionality reductions, based on Birkhoff--Hilbert contractions of the projective metric on a positive cone.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Identifying Implicit Bias in LLM-based Chat AI Toward People with Intellectual Disabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26062)
*Karly V. Coffey,Gloria L. Krahn,John P. Hanley,Jacob E. Neely*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究使用GPT-4-Turbo等五个LLM生成了25,000个故事，发现AI在描述智力障碍者时存在隐性偏见，包括将其描绘得更年轻、更依赖他人、更符号化，以及包含家长主义和消极态度等倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于LLM的聊天AI是否对智力障碍者存在隐性偏见，因为此前已有研究关注AI对其他群体的偏见，但对智力障碍群体的系统性偏见研究尚不充分。

Method: 使用GPT-4-Turbo等五个LLM，基于10个故事提示词（含/不含智力障碍描述）生成25,000个故事，再通过独立的GPT-4-Turbo实例分析两类故事数据集之间的表征差异。

Result: 有智力障碍描述的故事中，该群体被描绘得更年轻、更符号化、更依赖帮助、更被动（被拯救），同时隐含家长主义、幼稚化、消极感知和犹豫接纳等偏见。

Conclusion: LLM对智力障碍者存在超越其固有特征的负面隐性偏见，反映了历史上的歧视模式，亟需在AI开发中加强对此类偏见的评估与缓解，以防止未来社会危害。

Abstract: Background: This work investigates the presence of implicit bias in Large Language Model (LLM)-based chat AI models directed toward people with intellectual disabilities (ID).
  Objective: The study aims to identify and measure representational differences related to people with ID and examine them to identify implicit biases inherent in AI chat generation technologies.
  Methods: Utilizing the GPT-4-Turbo model, we requested story-generation based on 10 prompt stems with and without descriptors for ID. This process was repeated using four other LLMs (OpenAI GPT-4o, Meta Llama-3-3-70B-Instruct, Anthropic Claude-3-5-Sonnet, and Mistral-Large-2411). The resulting 25,000 computer-generated stories were analyzed using a separate GPT-4-Turbo model instance to detect differences in how people are represented related to themes of bias described in previous literature.
  Results: Our findings reveal differences in how people are represented between story datasets with and without ID descriptors. These differences go beyond established characteristics of ID and imply the presence of mostly negative implicit biases. Identified differences related to considering people with ID as younger, with themes of paternalism and infantilization; depicting them as more inspirational and symbolic; as needing help more often, being dependent, and being saved; and having a negative perception of them and more hesitation to include them.
  Conclusions: These implicit biases are considered within the context of past discrimination towards people with ID and highlight the need for diligence against implicit bias towards people with ID in AI development. This research underscores the importance of assessing and mitigating implicit bias in decision-making technologies to prevent future societal harm.

</details>


### [224] [Archetypes or ability? Clustering for modelling student mathematical competence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26063)
*Benjamin Mawdsley,Tom Quilter,Richard Turner,Sarah Jackson,Paul Edwards*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过对英国13个国家级考试中119,034名学生的数据集进行聚类分析，发现学生数学能力的主要因素是整体能力水平，而非离散的技能集合，使用伯努利混合模型达到了78%的准确率，表明可解释模型在教育领域具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证个性化学习系统中关于数学能力由离散技能组成、按顺序获得且依赖基础能力的假设是否成立。

Method: 使用伯努利混合模型（Bernoulli Mixture Model）对119,034名学生的13个英国国家级考试数据进行聚类分析，将题目结果分为通过/失败，并与逻辑回归和K近邻基线模型进行比较。

Result: 数据中仅存在少数几个聚类，主导因素是学生的整体能力；最佳模型准确率达78%，与更复杂的模型竞争力相当；使用每个题目表现作为特征时，性能略有提升。

Conclusion: 整体能力水平是预测表现的主导因素，学生在不同主题间未发展出显著差异化的能力；但针对学生具体优势进行个性化调整仍可带来微小改进，可解释模型在教育领域能达到竞争性性能。

Abstract: Personalised learning systems often assume that mathematical ability is combined of discrete abilities, acquired sequentially and dependent upon first acquiring foundational abilities, and students often report different strengths. In this work, we explore the validity of these assumptions by applying clustering methods to a large dataset of 119,034 students, spanning 13 national-level exams sat in the United Kingdom and collected by the platform. Classifying question results as pass or fail, we use a Bernoulli Mixture Model to search for latent populations which would be indicative of discrete skill-sets. We find that few distinct clusters are present in the data and that the dominant factor is overall student ability, which is further supported by the high degree of linear correlation between the probability distributions of the resulting clusters. Our best performing model achieves an accuracy of 78 percent, competitive with more complicated models in the literature whilst being more explainable. Comparing this models performance with logistic regression baselines and with k-nearest neighbours, we find a small improvement when using performance on each individual question as features. This suggests that whilst overall ability level is the dominant factor for predicting performance, small further personalisation improvements can be made by tailoring to a students exact strengths, but that students do not appear to develop strongly differing ability across topics. Our work offers a national scale test of machine learning in education and offers a new benchmark for the field, demonstrating how explainable models can reach competitive performance

</details>


### [225] [The Age of AI Agents Demands A New Scientific Paradigm To Sustain Trustworthy Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26064)
*Belinda Mo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文指出，随着AI科研代理的快速发展，人类验证科学输出的能力与科研产出之间出现巨大鸿沟，呼吁建立适应AI时代的新型验证基础设施，强调默认可观察工作流、可扩展验证和清晰归属，否则将面临不可验证的结果、优化指标而非理解以及责任真空等危险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统正成为自主研究代理，能够生成假设、设计实验并产生超越人类监督规模的发现。机器学习领域的投稿量激增已显示出验证差距在扩大，而自主代理由于人类与代理之间的不对称性使这一问题呈数量级恶化，因此亟需对科学验证基础设施进行适应性改革。

Method: 本文提出了一套适应性验证基础设施的标准，包括：(1) 默认可观察的工作流（observable-by-default workflows）；(2) 可扩展验证（scalable verification）；(3) 清晰归属（clear attribution）。这些标准针对AI代理缺乏可被问责和制裁的人类贡献者这一核心挑战而设计。

Result: 文章指出，如果不进行适应性改革，ML及任何使用AI代理的科学领域将面临三类危险：实验结果无人能够验证、优化指标凌驾于对真实理解之上、以及责任真空侵蚀科学信任。

Conclusion: 科学必须像历史上引入同行评审那样，主动演进其验证基础设施。但过去的基础设施假设人类贡献者能够被质疑和制裁，而AI代理打破了这一假设，因此需要全新的适应性方案来维护科学的可信度与完整性。

Abstract: AI systems are becoming autonomous research agents that generate hypotheses, design experiments, and produce discoveries at scales beyond human oversight. As seen by increased submissions to ML venues, the verification gap between scientific output and our ability to check it is already widening, and autonomous agents make it worse by magnitudes given human-agent asymmetry. We argue that science must evolve its verification infrastructure, as it has before with peer review. However, while historical adaptations assumed human contributors who could be questioned and sanctioned, AI agents break this assumption. We propose criteria for an adapted verification infrastructure that emphasizes observable-by-default workflows, scalable verification, and clear attribution. We argue that without adaptation, ML and any scientific domain using agents face dangerous failures: experimental results that no person can verify, optimization for metrics over understanding, and accountability vacuums that erode scientific trust.

</details>


### [226] [The Easy Trap: Why LLMs Underestimate Misconception-Driven Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26067)
*Amanda La Hadi,Muhammad Johan Alibasa,Guanliang Chen,A. Taufiq Asyhari*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究探究LLM对题目难度的评估是否与学生的实际表现一致。结果表明，LLM能大致排序难度，但在分数题上存在系统性偏差，容易将学生觉得难的题目误判为简单，即"Easy Trap"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于评估教育测试中的题目难度，但其评估能否真实反映学习者的实际体验尚不清楚。本文旨在检验LLM难度评分与真实学生表现之间的一致性。

Method: 使用四种LLM系统对32道算术题进行1-100难度评分（共640个评分），并与770名印尼大学生的实际答题数据（CTT和2PL模型）进行对比分析。

Result: Spearman秩相关系数为0.52-0.70，LLM能大致排序题目难度；但在分数题上存在系统性偏差，例如一道"100 : 1/2"的题目仅34.16%的学生答对，却被LLM评为简单。

Conclusion: LLM评估的是课程难度（基于教学顺序的"应该容易"），而非认知难度（基于学习者的误解），导致对误解驱动型题目难度的系统性低估。仅靠LLM估计难度而不做实证验证，会给评估设计和自适应系统带来偏差。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for estimating item difficulty in educational assessment. However, it remains unclear whether such estimates reflect how learners actually experience difficulty. This study investigates the alignment between LLM-generated difficulty ratings and empirical student performance on basic mathematics tasks. Four widely used LLM-based systems generated difficulty ratings on a 1-100 scale for 32 arithmetic items across multiple runs (N = 640 ratings). These were compared with empirical difficulty derived from responses of 770 Indonesian undergraduates using Classical Test Theory (CTT) and Item Response Theory (2PL). Results show moderate rank correlations (Spearman's rho = 0.52-0.70), indicating that LLMs capture coarse ordering of item difficulty. However, substantial and systematic misalignment emerges in fraction items. Several items consistently rated as easy by LLMs were among the most difficult for students, such as an item with only 34.16% correct for 100 : 1/2. We argue that LLMs approximate curricular difficulty, or what should be easy based on instructional sequencing, rather than cognitive difficulty driven by learner misconceptions. This leads to systematic underestimation of misconception-driven items, a phenomenon we term the Easy Trap. These findings highlight a critical limitation of LLM-based difficulty estimation and suggest that relying on such estimates without empirical grounding may introduce bias in assessment design and adaptive systems.

</details>


### [227] [AI Security Priorities: A Field-Wide Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26069)
*Gil Gekker,Rachel Steratore,Everett Smith,Asher Brass-Gershovich,Varun Gandhi,Nicole Nichols,Vijay Bolina,Buck Shlegeris,Lisa Einstein,Dan Lahav,Omer Nevo,Sella Nevo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过多领域专家访谈和研讨会，提出了一份优先级的AI安全行动议程，涵盖政策框架、公私协调、技术工程和自主AI治理四大主题，旨在为各方提供可操作的投资与行动基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在关键领域的快速部署与AI安全准备之间存在日益扩大的差距，亟需一份系统性的、优先级明确的行动纲领来指导各方协同推进AI安全。

Method: 采用结构化访谈（产业、政府、民间社会领袖）与多部门专家研讨会相结合的方式，识别并排序了高重要性、高成本效益的AI安全优先领域。

Result: 形成了四大主题的优先级议程：战略基础与政策框架、公私协调与制度基础设施、技术安全工程与保障、对抗压力下的自主AI治理。每个领域均包含问题定义、现状评估和可行动项目。

Conclusion: 本文为AI安全领域提供了一个实用的初始行动基础，旨在促进各方协调投资与行动，同时为不同背景的实践者提供切入点。

Abstract: As AI systems are rapidly integrated into critical economic, governmental, and national security functions, the gap between AI adoption and AI security readiness continues to widen. This paper presents a prioritized agenda for advancing AI security, informed by structured interviews with leaders across industry, government, and civil society, and refined through a multi-sector expert workshop. Participants identified and ranked the highest-importance and most cost-effective areas where progress could strengthen AI security - from protecting frontier AI systems and their underlying infrastructure to improving cybersecurity practices as AI reshapes the threat landscape. The resulting priorities are organized across four themes: establishing strategic foundations and policy frameworks; advancing public-private coordination and institutional infrastructure; advancing technical security engineering and assurance; and governing agentic AI under adversarial pressure. For each priority area, expert authors provide detailed analyses that define the problem, assess the current landscape, and identify actionable projects that stakeholders across sectors can pursue. The paper aims to serve as an initial practical foundation for coordinated investment and action across the AI security field. It is designed to serve both current practitioners and individuals and organizations looking to enter the field by identifying concrete, high-impact contributions suited to a range of strengths and capacities.

</details>


### [228] [Aligning LLM-Simulated and Human Examinees for Psychometric Calibration: A Cognitive Diagnostic Profiling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26317)
*Wenjie Zhou,Yunting Liu,Renjiao Tang,Mark Wilson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出认知诊断画像（CDP）框架，通过让大语言模型模拟具有多样认知特征的考生，显著提升了模拟考生与真实考生在能力分布、掌握模式和题目难度三个层面的心理测量对齐度，使LLM模拟考生可用于实际的测试开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理测量校准通常需要昂贵的人类响应数据，LLM模拟考生虽提供了早期校准的可行途径，但其响应过于准确且过于统一，无法真实反映人类考生的多样性。

Method: 提出CDP零样本框架，将二元属性掌握模式转化为自然语言描述，在无信息或有信息分布下采样，生成具有多样认知特征的考生画像，引导LLM模拟不同水平的考生作答。

Result: 在Tatsuoka分数减法数据集上，CDP在三层面均实现提升：分布重叠度提高；画像级分数与人类画像期望的加权相关性达0.92-0.98；题目难度恢复在排序和绝对对齐上均有改善，Gemini 3.0 Flash (Thinking)的1PL难度Spearman相关性从0.24提升至0.86和0.90，RMSE从6.31降至1.30和0.90。

Conclusion: CDP使LLM模拟考生与真实考生的心理测量对齐度大幅提升，使其在实际测试开发中具备实用性价值。

Abstract: Psychometric calibration for educational tests typically requires costly human response data. Large language models (LLMs) simulated examinees offer a promising route to early calibration, but their responses are too accurate and too uniform. We propose Cognitive Diagnostic Profiling (CDP), a zero-shot framework that prompts LLMs to simulate plausible examinees with diverse cognitive profiles: binary attribute-mastery patterns are rendered as natural-language profiles and sampled under an uninformative or an informative distribution. Using the Tatsuoka fraction-subtraction dataset (536 examinees, 15 items, five attributes), we evaluated eight LLM configurations under no-profile, uninformative-CDP, and informative-CDP conditions, assessing alignment with human examinees at the ability-distribution, mastery-profile, and item-difficulty levels. CDP improved all three levels: distributional overlap rose across configurations; weighted correlations between profile-level scores and human profile expectations reached 0.92 to 0.98; and item-difficulty recovery improved in rank order and absolute alignment, most for reasoning-enabled models; in the strongest case, Gemini 3.0 Flash (Thinking), one-parameter logistic (1PL) difficulty Spearman correlations rose from 0.24 to 0.86 and 0.90 and the root-mean-square error (RMSE) fell from 6.31 to 1.30 and 0.90; the informative condition helped most where profile-level alignment was strong. CDP brings LLM-simulated examinees into closer psychometric alignment with human examinees, making them practical for operational test development.

</details>


### [229] ["Nobody Did This": Contribution, Originality, and Accountability in Agent-Mediated Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26387)
*Kashif Imteyaz,Mohammad Rashidujjaman Rifat,Divya Ramesh,Steven R. Rick,Simo Hosio,Hauke Sandhaus,Advait Sarkar,Christoph Riedl,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LLM智能体嵌入团队协作知识工作后导致的"贡献消解"问题——归因、原创性和问责制的模糊化，并提出了一个研究议程来应对这一挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体不仅促进协作，还在贡献形成的时刻直接介入工作流程，这破坏了贡献被见证、归因和问责的社会条件，因此需要深入研究这种新现象。

Method: 通过立场陈述、映射练习和动手活动，让参与者揭示将问责制视为文档问题（如AI使用声明、水印、溯源日志）的局限性。

Result: 旨在产生一个共享研究议程和基础设施响应的基础，以应对协作知识工作中的贡献消解问题。

Conclusion: 贡献消解始于个体工作者对自己产出的不确定性，并通过协作关系传播，瓦解了富有成效的知识交流所需的可靠性；仅靠技术文档手段不足以解决问责制问题。

Abstract: Collaborative knowledge work is changing in ways that go beyond disclosure or transparency. LLM agents are now embedded in how teams research, design, write, and decide: mediating between members, synthesizing inputs, reformulating ideas, and drafting shared outputs. They do not only facilitate collaboration; they operate within the workflow at the moment contributions are being formed. In doing so, they risk undermining the social conditions under which contributions can be witnessed, attributed, and held accountable. This workshop brings together researchers and practitioners to confront what we call contribution dissolution: the blurring of attribution, originality, and accountability in agent-mediated collaborative work. We argue that this dissolution begins before collaboration itself, in the individual worker's own uncertainty about what is genuinely theirs, and propagates through collaborative relationships, collapsing the reliability that makes productive intellectual exchange possible. Through position statements, mapping exercises, and a hands-on activity, participants will surface how framing accountability as a documentation problem (e.g., AI use statements, watermarking, provenance logs) overlooks the conditions under which accountability is produced. Our goal is to produce a shared research agenda and the foundations of an infrastructural response to contribution dissolution in collaborative knowledge work.

</details>


### [230] [From Micro-Cognition to Self-Construction: A Four-Layer Integrative Review of Psychological Theories in HCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26402)
*Xiaohe Bie,Bo Wang,Xinyu Long*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出人机交互从"工具使用"转向"伙伴关系"乃至"精神共生"的范式变革，构建了包含微观认知层、中观情感层、宏观社会层和自我建构层的四层整合框架，揭示了心理学从"事后评估"到"主动设计"的角色转变，并讨论了生成式AI、具身智能等新兴场景下的挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互正经历从"工具使用"到"伙伴关系"乃至"心灵共生"的范式转变，心理学在其中的角色日益重要，但缺乏系统性的理论框架来整合认知、情感、社会与自我建构等多个层面。本文旨在填补这一空白，构建一个多层次理论框架以理解复杂的人机关系。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，梳理相关研究后提出四层整合框架（微观认知层、中观情感层、宏观社会层、自我建构层），并论证各层之间的递进逻辑关系（"基础—中介—情境—目标"）。

Result: 揭示了四个层面的核心作用：认知层是交互基础，情感层驱动关系参与，社会层通过规范调节信任与行为，自我建构层指向人机共生的终极目标；明确了心理学从"事后评估"向"主动设计范式"转变的趋势。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解复杂人机关系提供了系统性理论视角，同时识别出情感本质、能动性侵蚀、伦理风险等核心挑战，并提出了双向心智理论、个性化协同进化等未来研究方向。

Abstract: Human-computer interaction (HCI) is undergoing a paradigm shift from "tool use" toward "partnership" and even "mind symbiosis," with psychology evolving from a supplementary explanatory tool to a core pillar shaping interaction paradigms and long-term relationships. This paper systematically reviews relevant research and proposes a four-layer integrative framework comprising the Micro-cognitive, Meso-affective, Macro-social, and Self-constructive layers. The framework reveals that: the cognitive layer constitutes the foundation of interaction, the affective layer drives relational engagement, the social layer regulates trust and behavior through norms, and the self-constructive layer points to the ultimate goal of human-machine symbiosis. These four layers follow a progressive logic of "foundation--mediation--context--goal." The review further argues that psychology has shifted from "post-hoc evaluation" to a "proactive design paradigm," with direct implications for emerging scenarios such as generative AI and embodied agents. Concurrently, this paper identifies core challenges including the nature of affect, agency erosion, and ethical risks, and proposes future directions such as bidirectional theory of mind and personalized co-evolution. This framework offers a systematic theoretical perspective for understanding complex human-machine relationships.

</details>


### [231] [Anticipatory Data Governance in the Age of AI: Emerging Signals in Data Access, Reuse, and Sovereignty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27029)
*Adam Zable,Stefaan Verhulst*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过2025-2026年两轮专家预见性研究，识别出数据治理领域七大汇聚信号，包括开放数据范式承压、生态系统向机器中心化与AI中介化转变、推理重塑治理基础、基础设施难以维系、治理碎片化、主权安全驱动战略控制、以及数据共享需要更强激励。论文主张数据治理正与AI治理、数字公共基础设施、经济战略、民主韧性和地缘竞争密不可分，并提出前瞻性治理议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对数据访问、治理和再利用领域的快速变化，现有治理框架可能滞后于技术与社会发展。研究者希望通过结构化前瞻方法，系统识别正在发生的结构性转变，避免依赖、风险和错失机会被锁定。

Method: 采用基于地平线扫描和前瞻治理传统的定性信号扫描方法，组织了两次专家预测研讨会，汇集19位来自官方统计、数字与贸易政策、开放科学、AI治理、地理空间系统和公共部门创新等领域的资深从业者，对新兴发展进行 elicitation、聚类和主题综合，并通过从业者经验进行压力测试。

Result: 识别出七大汇聚信号：(1)开放数据范式承压；(2)数据生态向机器中心化和AI中介化转变；(3)推理重塑数据治理基础；(4)数据基础设施难以维系；(5)治理在机构和司法管辖区间碎片化；(6)主权和安全驱动战略控制转向；(7)数据共享模式需要更强激励和利益分享机制。同时绘制了这些信号之间的增强反馈回路。

Conclusion: 数据治理正变得与AI治理、数字公共基础设施、经济战略、民主韧性和地缘竞争不可分割。研究贡献在于提供诊断性框架以推理已发生的结构性转变，而非预测特定技术结果，并提出了适应性的前瞻性数据治理议程。

Abstract: This paper reports findings from a structured participatory foresight study comprising two expert forecasting studios convened by The GovLab between 2025 and 2026. The studios brought together nineteen senior practitioners spanning official statistics, digital and trade policy, open science, AI governance, geospatial systems, and public-sector innovation across multiple jurisdictions. Applying a qualitative signal-scanning methodology grounded in the horizon-scanning and anticipatory-governance traditions, we elicited, clustered, and thematically synthesized emerging developments in data access, governance,and reuse, and stress-tested them against practitioner experience. We identify seven convergent signals: (1) the open-data paradigm is under strain; (2) data ecosystems are becoming machine-centric and AI-mediated; (3) inference is reshaping the foundations of data governance;(4) data infrastructure is becoming harder to sustain; (5) governance is fragmenting across institutions and jurisdictions; (6) sovereignty and security are driving a turn toward strategic control; and (7) data-sharing models require stronger incentives and benefit-sharing mechanisms. We further map the reinforcing feedback loops that couple these signals, showing how interventions in one domain propagate risks and opportunities across the wider ecosystem. We argue that data governance is becoming inseparable from AI governance, digital public infrastructure, economic strategy, democratic resilience, and geopolitical competition, and we outline an agenda for anticipatory data governance capable of adapting before dependencies, risks, and missed opportunities become locked in. The contribution is diagnostic rather than predictive: the signals offer an evidence-informed framework for reasoning about structural shifts already underway, not a forecast of specific technological outcomes.

</details>


### [232] [Can Large Language Models Represent Urban Publics? Behavioral Replication and Population Mismatch in an Affordable-Housing Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27100)
*Yuxuan Cai,Yequan Hu,Hongqian Li,Zhanghong Ju,Shuying Guo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究测试了8个开源大语言模型能否模拟美国保障房调查实验中居民态度的空间锚定与身份条件化结构。结果显示，Qwen 2.5 14B在总体平均效应上最接近人类（-0.242 vs -0.285），但在群体异质性、测量稳定性等方面存在严重结构性失败，表明仅关注平均效应的评估方式存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM能预测调查实验的平均结果，但对其能否保留空间锚定、身份条件化结构（如项目靠近住宅时支持度的变化，以及不同产权、党派群体之间的分化）知之甚少。本文旨在系统检验这一结构性保真度。

Method: 将8个开源LLM与843名人类受访者在"保障房距2英里vs 1/8英里"这一空间距离实验中的反应进行比较，预设+/-0.20等价性标准，并从群体异质性（产权×党派）、测量稳定性（题目顺序效应、身份线索移除、理由优先vs选择优先）等多维度评估。

Result: Qwen 2.5 14B在总体平均效应上唯一满足等价性标准（-0.242 vs -0.285）；但结构性失败显著：弱化了共和党效应、夸大了独立选民效应；27个条件组合下RMSE达0.613；中位数模型-人类方差比仅0.099；题目顺序改变效应量达+0.367；身份线索移除使部分比较不可估；20.6-35.3%的焦点比较在理由优先与直接选择之间存在差异。

Conclusion: LLM可能近似某一总体平均对比，但无法保真地再现生成该对比的人口结构、组内异质性和测量稳定性，建议城市建模的LLM评估应检验空间与社会结构的存活情况，而非仅关注平均效应。

Abstract: There is growing interest in using large language models (LLMs) as low-cost proxies for resident attitudes in urban planning. Previous work shows that LLMs can predict average results of survey experiments, but less is known about whether they preserve the spatially anchored, identity-conditioned structure behind those averages, namely how support changes as a project approaches homes and how that response divides across tenure and partisan groups. We compared eight open-weight LLMs with 843 respondents in a US affordable-housing survey experiment, testing whether they reproduced the owner-renter difference in support change as a proposed development moved from 2 miles to 1/8 mile. Qwen 2.5 14B was closest (-0.242 versus the human -0.285) and was the only model to meet the prespecified +/-0.20 equivalence criterion; Phi-4 14B was directionally aligned but attenuated (-0.150), and other models showed weak, null, or reversed moderation. This aggregate match masked structural failure. Qwen attenuated the Republican contrast and exaggerated the Independent one, its RMSE across 27 party-by-tenure-by-item cells was 0.613, its median model-to-human variance ratio was 0.099, and question order shifted the contrast by +0.367. Identity-cue removal and selective nonresponse changed which comparisons were estimable, and rationale-first responses differed from matched direct-choice responses in 20.6-35.3% of focal comparisons. An LLM can thus approximate one aggregate contrast while failing to preserve the population structure, within-group heterogeneity, and measurement stability that generate it. Model evaluation in urban planning should test whether this spatial and social structure survives simulation, not only average effects.

</details>


### [233] [It Doesn't Take a Thief: Optical-Scan Voting Systems Fail Even Without Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27101)
*Aleksander Essex,Philip B. Stark*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对光学扫描投票系统中的非对抗性故障模式进行了分类，包括纸质选票记录、读取、汇总计算以及测试验证四个环节，并指出现有纸质审计机制无法检测或纠正如错误选票样式、设备记录选票及选票保管不当等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究关注投票系统的对抗性威胁，但许多司法管辖区仍未充分认识到计票器本身可能存在的错误。利益相关者往往对假设性攻击不以为然，但对真实发生的设备和流程故障案例更有感触。

Method: 本文提出了一套非对抗性故障模式的分类法，按照投票流程分为四个直观类别：纸质选票上的投票记录、从纸质选票读取投票、汇总读取的投票以生成报告结果、以及测试与验证。每类均用有记录的真实事件加以说明。

Result: 研究发现，现有常见的验证机制在该分类法中存在漏洞，某些故障无法被任何纸质审计手段检测或纠正，最突出的是那些损害纸质凭证可信度的故障，包括：给选民错误的选票样式（遗漏其应参与的选举或包含其不应参与的选举）、使用选票标记设备记录选票、以及未能妥善保管和整理已投选票。

Conclusion: 非对抗性故障是投票系统中不可忽视的风险源，现有审计机制存在结构性盲区，尤其当纸质凭证本身已被污染时。这要求我们在关注外部攻击的同时，也应重视系统内部固有的可靠性问题。

Abstract: Optical-scan voting systems and their supporting ecosystem of people, processes, and technology are fallible. While a substantial body of work examines adversarial threats to such systems, we have encountered jurisdictions where the possibility of tabulator error is not fully internalized. Stakeholders there often find hypothetical attacks unconvincing, but some are persuaded by real-world accounts of equipment and procedural failures. This paper introduces a taxonomy of non-adversarial failure modes organized into intuitive categories: recording votes on paper, reading votes from the paper, combining votes as read into a reported outcome, and testing and verifying, all illustrated with documented incidents. We map common verification mechanisms against this taxonomy, identifying gaps that no paper-based audit can detect or correct, most notably failures that compromise the trustworthiness of the paper trail, such as giving voters the wrong ballot style (omitting contests they are eligible for, or including ones they are not), using ballot-marking devices to record votes, or failing to keep voted ballots secure and organized.

</details>
